# Command & Conquer Generals (inc. Zero Hour) Source Code

This repository includes source code for Command & Conquer Generals, and its expansion pack Zero Hour. This release provides support to the Steam Workshop for both games ([C&C Generals](https://steamcommunity.com/workshop/browse/?appid=2229870) and [C&C Generals - Zero Hour](https://steamcommunity.com/workshop/browse/?appid=2732960)).


## Dependencies

If you wish to rebuild the source code and tools successfully you will need to find or write new replacements (or remove the code using them entirely) for the following libraries;

- DirectX SDK (Version 9.0 or higher) (expected path `\Code\Libraries\DirectX\`)
- STLport (4.5.3) - (expected path `\Code\Libraries\STLport-4.5.3`)
- 3DSMax 4 SDK - (expected path `\Code\Libraries\Max4SDK\`)
- NVASM - (expected path `\Code\Tools\NVASM\`)
- BYTEmark - (expected path `\Code\Libraries\Source\Benchmark`)
- RAD Miles Sound System SDK - (expected path `\Code\Libraries\Source\WWVegas\Miles6\`)
- RAD Bink SDK - (expected path `\Code\GameEngineDevice\Include\VideoDevice\Bink`)
- SafeDisk API - (expected path `\Code\GameEngine\Include\Common\SafeDisk` and `\Code\Tools\Launcher\SafeDisk\`)
- Miles Sound System "Asimp3" - (expected path `\Code\Libraries\WPAudio\Asimp3`)
- GameSpy SDK - (expected path `\Code\Libraries\Source\GameSpy\`)
- ZLib (1.1.4) - (expected path `\Code\Libraries\Source\Compression\ZLib\`)
- LZH-Light (1.0) - (expected path `\Code\Libraries\Source\Compression\LZHCompress\CompLibSource` and `CompLibHeader`)


## Compiling (Win32 Only)

To use the compiled binaries, you must own the game. The C&C Ultimate Collection is available for purchase on [EA App](https://www.ea.com/en-gb/games/command-and-conquer/command-and-conquer-the-ultimate-collection/buy/pc) or [Steam](https://store.steampowered.com/bundle/39394/Command__Conquer_The_Ultimate_Collection/).

The quickest way to build all configurations in the project is to open `rts.dsw` in Microsoft Visual Studio C++ 6.0 (SP6 recommended for binary matching to Generals patch 1.08 and Zero Hour patch 1.04) and select Build -> Batch Build, then hit the “Rebuild All” button.

If you wish to compile the code under a modern version of Microsoft Visual Studio, you can convert the legacy project file to a modern MSVC solution by opening `rts.dsw` in Microsoft Visual Studio .NET 2003, and then opening the newly created project and solution file in MSVC 2015 or newer.

NOTE: As modern versions of MSVC enforce newer revisions of the C++ standard, you will need to make extensive changes to the codebase before it successfully compiles, even more so if you plan on compiling for the Win64 platform.

When the workspace has finished building, the compiled binaries will be copied to the folder called `/Run/` found in the root of each games directory. 


## Known Issues

Windows has a policy where executables that contain words “version”, “update” or “install” in their filename will require UAC Elevation to run. This will affect “versionUpdate” and “buildVersionUpdate” projects from running as post-build events. Renaming the output binary name for these projects to not include these words should resolve the issue for you.


## STLport
STLport will require changes to successfully compile this source code. The file [stlport.diff](stlport.diff) has been provided for you so you can review and apply these changes. Please make sure you are using STLport 4.5.3 before attempting to apply the patch.


## Contributing

This repository will not be accepting contributions (pull requests, issues, etc). If you wish to create changes to the source code and encourage collaboration, please create a fork of the repository under your GitHub user/organization space.

> AGENT

**Intel Mac. No way around it.**

The plan itself says it explicitly:

> "Architecture: ...The engine runs on Windows (Intel Mac VM) because it remains deeply Win32-tied (ATL, HINSTANCE, timeGetTime, windows.h in every TU)."

And the README confirms: **"Win32 Only"** — requires MSVC, DirectX 9, and the original ZH game files for INI loading.

The core blockers on M4:
- `windows.h` / Win32 API in every translation unit — not portable
- DirectX 9 headers (Windows SDK only)
- `timeGetTime`, `HINSTANCE`, `ATL` throughout
- Requires MSVC (the README even recommends VS 6.0 SP6 for binary matching)
- The game's `.big` asset files needed for INI loading at runtime

**What you need on Intel Mac:**
- Windows 10/11 via Parallels, VMware Fusion, or Boot Camp
- Visual Studio 2019+ (Community is free)
- Original ZH game installed (for asset files)
- Python 3.11 + pybind11 + pyzmq

**What you CAN do on M4 right now:**
- The Python-side ZMQ client (`src/engine/python_client/`) — connects to the Windows engine server
- The gym environment wrapper
- Protocol/data structure definitions

But the C++ engine work itself is gated on having Windows running on the Intel Mac. Get that set up first.

1	# Plan 2: Replay Parser & Dataset Pipeline
2	
3	> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.
4	
5	**Goal:** Parse C&C Generals Zero Hour `.rep` replay files into structured Python data — command sequences, player info, game options. Then (on Intel Mac) replay them through the headless engine to generate `(state, action, outcome)` training datasets.
6	
7	**Architecture:** Pure Python parser reads `.rep` binary format → `ReplayFile` dataclass. A separate `ReplayRunner` (engine-dependent, Intel Mac only) replays commands through the headless engine and records `GameStateObservation` at each decision step. Datasets are stored as HDF5 files.
8	
9	**Tech Stack:** Python 3.11+, `struct`, `h5py`, `numpy`, `pyarrow` (Parquet), `tqdm`, `pytest`
10	
11	**M4 Mac scope:** Tasks 1–5 (parser, game options, analysis tools, collection scripts, tests).
12	**Intel Mac scope:** Tasks 6–7 (replay runner, dataset generation — requires Plan 1 engine).
13	
14	---
15	
16	## File Structure
17	
18	```
19	src/zh_copilot/replay/
20	├── __init__.py
21	├── parser.py           # Binary .rep parser → ReplayFile dataclass
22	├── game_options.py     # gameOptions ASCII string sub-parser → GameInfo
23	├── message_types.py    # GameMessage type enum (1000-1999)
24	├── analysis.py         # Stats, filtering, quality checks on parsed replays
25	├── collector.py        # Download/scrape replay files from web sources
26	├── runner.py           # (Intel Mac) Replay via headless engine → HDF5 dataset
27	└── dataset.py          # HDF5 dataset reader for training pipeline
28	
29	tests/replay/
30	├── __init__.py
31	├── fixtures/           # Sample .rep files for testing (small, committed)
32	│   └── sample.rep      # minimal valid replay for unit tests
33	├── test_parser.py
34	├── test_game_options.py
35	└── test_analysis.py
36	```
37	
38	---
39	
40	## .rep File Format Reference
41	
42	### Header (fixed section, bytes 0–27)
43	
44	| Offset | Field | Type | Size |
45	|--------|-------|------|------|
46	| 0x00 | magic | ASCII | 6 bytes — `"GENREP"` |
47	| 0x06 | start_time | uint32 | 4 bytes — Unix timestamp |
48	| 0x0A | end_time | uint32 | 4 bytes — Unix timestamp |
49	| 0x0E | frame_duration | uint32 | 4 bytes — total logic frames |
50	| 0x12 | desync_game | bool | 1 byte |
51	| 0x13 | quit_early | bool | 1 byte |
52	| 0x14 | player_discons[0..7] | bool×8 | 8 bytes |
53	
54	### Header (variable section, immediately after byte 27)
55	
56	| Field | Encoding |
57	|-------|----------|
58	| replay_name | Null-terminated UTF-16LE |
59	| time_val | 16 bytes — SYSTEMTIME (8×uint16: year, month, dow, day, hour, min, sec, ms) |
60	| version_string | Null-terminated UTF-16LE |
61	| version_time_string | Null-terminated UTF-16LE |
62	| version_number | uint32 |
63	| exe_crc | uint32 |
64	| ini_crc | uint32 |
65	| game_options | Null-terminated ASCII |
66	| local_player_index | Null-terminated ASCII (decimal integer string) |
67	| difficulty | int32 |
68	| original_game_mode | int32 |
69	| rank_points | int32 |
70	| max_fps | int32 |
71	
72	### Command stream (immediately after header)
73	
74	Each command record:
75	```
76	frame:       uint32 (4 bytes)
77	type:        uint32 (4 bytes) — GameMessage::Type enum
78	player_idx:  int32  (4 bytes)
79	num_types:   uint8  (1 byte)  — number of arg type descriptors
80	then (num_types) times:

But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.
</system-reminder>

> AGENT

Phase 2 splits cleanly into two halves:

**M4 Mac (can start now — pure Python):**
- Tasks 1–5: Binary `.rep` file parser, game options sub-parser, message type enum, analysis tools, collection scripts
- Only needs: `h5py`, `numpy`, `pyarrow`, `tqdm`, `pytest`
- **Blocker:** Need actual `.rep` files to test against. You need to grab some from GameReplays.org, CnCNet, or Steam Workshop

**Intel Mac (blocked on Phase 1):**
- Tasks 6–7: `ReplayRunner` (feeds commands into headless engine) and HDF5 dataset generation
- Can't touch until the headless engine is running on Windows

**So the real question is: do you have any `.rep` replay files?**

Without them you can write the parser code but can't verify it parses correctly. The plan has a `tests/replay/fixtures/sample.rep` — that needs a real file committed to the repo.

Options to get replays:
1. **GameReplays.org** — large archive of ZH replays, downloadable
2. **CnCNet** — active community, replays section
3. **Play a game yourself** — ZH saves replays automatically to `%APPDATA%\Command & Conquer Generals Zero Hour\Replays\`

If you have ZH installed anywhere (or can get a replay file from someone), we can start Tasks 1–5 on M4 right now. Otherwise it's blocked on that asset too.

88	| Type value | Name | Bytes each |

100	| 10 | WIDECHAR | 2 |
101	| 11 | UNKNOWN | 0 |
102	
103	Network message range: 1000–1999. CRC messages (type=1000) appear every 100 frames — skip for gameplay analysis.

115	# src/zh_copilot/replay/message_types.py
116	"""GameMessage type enum values from MessageStream.h.
117	
118	Only messages in range MSG_BEGIN_NETWORK_MESSAGES (1000) to
119	MSG_END_NETWORK_MESSAGES (1999) are recorded in .rep files.
120	"""
121	
122	from enum import IntEnum
123	
124	
125	class ArgType(IntEnum):
126	    """GameMessageArgumentDataType — argument encoding types."""
127	    INTEGER     = 0
128	    REAL        = 1
129	    BOOLEAN     = 2
130	    OBJECTID    = 3
131	    DRAWABLEID  = 4
132	    TEAMID      = 5
133	    LOCATION    = 6   # Coord3D: 3 × float32 = 12 bytes
134	    PIXEL       = 7   # ICoord2D: 2 × int32 = 8 bytes
135	    PIXELREGION = 8   # IRegion2D: 4 × int32 = 16 bytes
136	    TIMESTAMP   = 9
137	    WIDECHAR    = 10
138	    UNKNOWN     = 11
139	
140	
141	# Bytes per argument for each ArgType
142	ARG_SIZE: dict[ArgType, int] = {
143	    ArgType.INTEGER:     4,
144	    ArgType.REAL:        4,
145	    ArgType.BOOLEAN:     1,
146	    ArgType.OBJECTID:    4,
147	    ArgType.DRAWABLEID:  4,

270	# tests/replay/test_parser.py
271	"""Tests for the .rep binary parser."""
272	
273	import io
274	import struct
275	import pytest
276	from pathlib import Path
277

279	    ReplayFile,

288	# --- Minimal valid .rep builder for tests ---
289	
290	def make_minimal_replay() -> bytes:
291	    """Build a minimal valid .rep binary in memory."""
292	    buf = io.BytesIO()
293	
294	    # Fixed header
295	    buf.write(b"GENREP")                    # magic (6)
296	    buf.write(struct.pack("<I", 1700000000))  # start_time (4)
297	    buf.write(struct.pack("<I", 1700003600))  # end_time   (4)
298	    buf.write(struct.pack("<I", 9000))        # frame_duration (4) — 9000 frames = 5 min
299	    buf.write(struct.pack("<?", False))       # desync_game (1)
300	    buf.write(struct.pack("<?", False))       # quit_early  (1)
301	    buf.write(bytes(8))                       # player_discons[0..7] (8) — all False
302	
303	    # Variable header: replay_name (UTF-16LE null-terminated)
304	    replay_name = "Test Replay\x00".encode("utf-16-le")
305	    buf.write(replay_name)
306	
307	    # time_val: SYSTEMTIME — 8×uint16 (year, month, dow, day, hour, min, sec, ms)
308	    buf.write(struct.pack("<8H", 2024, 1, 1, 15, 10, 30, 0, 0))
309	
310	    # version_string (UTF-16LE null-terminated)
311	    buf.write("1.04\x00".encode("utf-16-le"))
312	
313	    # version_time_string (UTF-16LE null-terminated)
314	    buf.write("Jan 15 2024 12:00:00\x00".encode("utf-16-le"))
315	
316	    # version_number, exe_crc, ini_crc
317	    buf.write(struct.pack("<III", 10401, 0xDEADBEEF, 0xCAFEBABE))
318	
319	    # game_options (ASCII null-terminated) — minimal valid string
320	    buf.write(b"M=maps/alpine.map C=1 F=America C=2 F=China\x00")
321	
322	    # local_player_index (ASCII null-terminated)
323	    buf.write(b"0\x00")
324	
325	    # difficulty, original_game_mode, rank_points, max_fps
326	    buf.write(struct.pack("<4i", 1, 7, 0, 30))  # normal, skirmish, 0 pts, 30fps
327	
328	    # One command: MSG_DO_MOVETO at frame 30, player 0
329	    # Args: one LOCATION (Coord3D) = 3 floats
330	    buf.write(struct.pack("<I", 30))          # frame
331	    buf.write(struct.pack("<I", MsgType.DO_MOVETO))  # type
332	    buf.write(struct.pack("<i", 0))           # player_idx
333	    buf.write(struct.pack("<B", 1))           # num_types = 1
334	    buf.write(struct.pack("<BB", ArgType.LOCATION, 1))  # argtype=LOCATION, count=1
335	    buf.write(struct.pack("<3f", 100.0, 200.0, 0.0))    # Coord3D
336	
337	    return buf.getvalue()
338	
339	
340	def test_magic_detection():
341	    data = make_minimal_replay()
342	    replay = parse_replay_bytes(data)
343	    assert replay.header.magic == "GENREP"
344	
345

425	```python
426	# src/zh_copilot/replay/parser.py
427	"""Binary parser for C&C Generals Zero Hour .rep replay files.
428	
429	Format documented in:
430	  cczh-source-code/GeneralsMD/Code/GameEngine/Source/Common/Recorder.cpp
431	
432	Usage:
433	    replay = parse_replay("path/to/game.rep")
434	    for cmd in replay.commands:
435	        print(cmd.frame, cmd.msg_type, cmd.player_idx)
436	"""
437	
438	from __future__ import annotations
439	
440	import struct
441	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
442	from pathlib import Path
443	from typing import Any
444	
445	from zh_copilot.replay.message_types import ArgType, ARG_SIZE, MsgType
446	
447	MAGIC = b"GENREP"
448	MAX_SLOTS = 8
449	
450	# Network message range — only these are recorded
451	MSG_NET_BEGIN = 1000

493	    @property

498	@dataclass
499	class ReplayCommand:
500	    """Single command record from the .rep command stream."""
501	    frame:      int
502	    msg_type:   int                            # raw int (may not be in MsgType enum)
503	    player_idx: int
504	    args:       dict[ArgType, list[Any]] = field(default_factory=dict)
505	
506	    @property
507	    def is_crc(self) -> bool:
508	        return self.msg_type == MSG_NET_BEGIN
509	
510	    @property
511	    def msg_type_name(self) -> str:
512	        try:
513	            return MsgType(self.msg_type).name
514	        except ValueError:
515	            return f"UNKNOWN_{self.msg_type}"
516	
517	
518	@dataclass
519	class ReplayFile:
520	    """Fully parsed .rep replay file."""
521	    header:   ReplayHeader
522	    commands: list[ReplayCommand]
523	
524	    @property
525	    def gameplay_commands(self) -> list[ReplayCommand]:
526	        """Commands excluding CRC sync messages."""
527	        return [c for c in self.commands if not c.is_crc]
528	
529	    @property
530	    def player_commands(self) -> dict[int, list[ReplayCommand]]:
531	        """Commands grouped by player index."""
532	        result: dict[int, list[ReplayCommand]] = {}
533	        for cmd in self.gameplay_commands:
534	            result.setdefault(cmd.player_idx, []).append(cmd)
535	        return result
536	
537	
538	class _BinaryReader:
539	    """Streaming binary reader over a bytes buffer."""
540	
541	    def __init__(self, data: bytes):
542	        self._data = data
543	        self._pos  = 0
544	
545	    @property
546	    def pos(self) -> int:
547	        return self._pos
548	
549	    @property
550	    def remaining(self) -> int:
551	        return len(self._data) - self._pos
552	
553	    def read(self, n: int) -> bytes:
554	        if self._pos + n > len(self._data):
555	            raise EOFError(f"Need {n} bytes at offset {self._pos}, only {self.remaining} remain")
556	        chunk = self._data[self._pos:self._pos + n]
557	        self._pos += n
558	        return chunk
559	
560	    def unpack(self, fmt: str) -> tuple:
561	        size = struct.calcsize(fmt)
562	        return struct.unpack(fmt, self.read(size))
563	
564	    def read_u8(self)  -> int:   return self.unpack("<B")[0]
565	    def read_i32(self) -> int:   return self.unpack("<i")[0]
566	    def read_u32(self) -> int:   return self.unpack("<I")[0]
567	    def read_bool(self) -> bool: return bool(self.unpack("<?")[0])
568	    def read_f32(self) -> float: return self.unpack("<f")[0]
569	
570	    def read_wstr(self) -> str:
571	        """Read null-terminated UTF-16LE string."""
572	        chars = []
573	        while True:
574	            word = self.unpack("<H")[0]
575	            if word == 0:
576	                break
577	            chars.append(word)
578	        return "".join(chr(c) for c in chars)
579	
580	    def read_astr(self) -> str:
581	        """Read null-terminated ASCII string."""
582	        chars = []
583	        while True:
584	            byte = self.read(1)[0]
585	            if byte == 0:
586	                break
587	            chars.append(chr(byte))
588	        return "".join(chars)
589	
590	
591	def _parse_header(r: _BinaryReader) -> ReplayHeader:
592	    # Fixed section (28 bytes)
593	    magic_bytes = r.read(6)
594	    if magic_bytes != MAGIC:
595	        raise ValueError(f"Invalid magic: expected {MAGIC!r}, got {magic_bytes!r}")
596	
597	    start_time     = r.read_u32()
598	    end_time       = r.read_u32()
599	    frame_duration = r.read_u32()
600	    desync_game    = r.read_bool()
601	    quit_early     = r.read_bool()
602	    player_discons = [r.read_bool() for _ in range(MAX_SLOTS)]
603	
604	    # Variable section
605	    replay_name         = r.read_wstr()
606	    time_val_raw        = r.unpack("<8H")
607	    time_val            = SystemTime(*time_val_raw)
608	    version_string      = r.read_wstr()
609	    version_time_string = r.read_wstr()
610	    version_number      = r.read_u32()
611	    exe_crc             = r.read_u32()
612	    ini_crc             = r.read_u32()
613	    game_options        = r.read_astr()
614	    local_player_str    = r.read_astr()
615	    local_player_index  = int(local_player_str) if local_player_str.lstrip("-").isdigit() else -1
616	    difficulty          = r.read_i32()
617	    original_game_mode  = r.read_i32()
618	    rank_points         = r.read_i32()
619	    max_fps             = r.read_i32()
620	
621	    return ReplayHeader(
622	        magic               = magic_bytes.decode("ascii"),
623	        start_time          = start_time,
624	        end_time            = end_time,
625	        frame_duration      = frame_duration,
626	        desync_game         = desync_game,
627	        quit_early          = quit_early,
628	        player_discons      = player_discons,
629	        replay_name         = replay_name,
630	        time_val            = time_val,
631	        version_string      = version_string,
632	        version_time_string = version_time_string,
633	        version_number      = version_number,
634	        exe_crc             = exe_crc,
635	        ini_crc             = ini_crc,
636	        game_options        = game_options,
637	        local_player_index  = local_player_index,
638	        difficulty          = difficulty,
639	        original_game_mode  = original_game_mode,
640	        rank_points         = rank_points,
641	        max_fps             = max_fps,
642	    )
643	
644	
645	def _parse_arg(r: _BinaryReader, arg_type: ArgType) -> Any:
646	    """Parse a single argument value of the given type."""
647	    size = ARG_SIZE[arg_type]
648	    if size == 0:
649	        return None
650	    if arg_type == ArgType.INTEGER:
651	        return r.read_i32()
652	    if arg_type == ArgType.REAL:
653	        return r.read_f32()
654	    if arg_type == ArgType.BOOLEAN:
655	        return r.read_bool()
656	    if arg_type in (ArgType.OBJECTID, ArgType.DRAWABLEID,
657	                    ArgType.TEAMID, ArgType.TIMESTAMP):
658	        return r.read_u32()
659	    if arg_type == ArgType.LOCATION:
660	        x, y, z = r.unpack("<3f")
661	        return (x, y, z)
662	    if arg_type == ArgType.PIXEL:
663	        x, y = r.unpack("<2i")
664	        return (x, y)
665	    if arg_type == ArgType.PIXELREGION:
666	        x1, y1, x2, y2 = r.unpack("<4i")
667	        return (x1, y1, x2, y2)
668	    if arg_type == ArgType.WIDECHAR:
669	        return chr(r.unpack("<H")[0])
670	    raw = r.read(size)
671	    return raw
672	
673	
674	def _parse_commands(r: _BinaryReader) -> list[ReplayCommand]:
675	    commands: list[ReplayCommand] = []
676	
677	    while r.remaining >= 4:
678	        try:
679	            frame = r.read_u32()
680	        except EOFError:
681	            break
682	
683	        if r.remaining < 9:   # type(4) + player(4) + num_types(1)
684	            break
685	
686	        msg_type   = r.read_u32()
687	        player_idx = r.read_i32()
688	        num_types  = r.read_u8()
689	
690	        # Terminal record: MSG_CLEAR_GAME_DATA (10) ends the stream
691	        if msg_type == MSG_CLEAR_GAME_DATA and num_types == 0:
692	            break
693	
694	        args: dict[ArgType, list[Any]] = {}
695	
696	        for _ in range(num_types):
697	            if r.remaining < 2:
698	                break
699	            raw_type  = r.read_u8()
700	            arg_count = r.read_u8()
701	
702	            try:
703	                arg_type = ArgType(raw_type)
704	            except ValueError:
705	                # Unknown arg type — skip if possible
706	                break
707	
708	            values = [_parse_arg(r, arg_type) for _ in range(arg_count)]
709	            args[arg_type] = values
710	
711	        commands.append(ReplayCommand(
712	            frame      = frame,
713	            msg_type   = msg_type,
714	            player_idx = player_idx,
715	            args       = args,
716	        ))
717	
718	    return commands
719	
720	
721	def parse_replay_bytes(data: bytes) -> ReplayFile:
722	    """Parse a .rep file from raw bytes. Raises ValueError on invalid format."""
723	    if not data:
724	        raise ValueError("Empty input")
725	    r = _BinaryReader(data)
726	    header   = _parse_header(r)
727	    commands = _parse_commands(r)
728	    return ReplayFile(header=header, commands=commands)
729	
730	
731	def parse_replay(path: str | Path) -> ReplayFile:
732	    """Parse a .rep file from disk."""
733	    data = Path(path).read_bytes()
734	    return parse_replay_bytes(data)
735	```

753	### Task 3: Game Options Sub-Parser
754	
755	**Files:**
756	- Create: `src/zh_copilot/replay/game_options.py`
757	- Create: `tests/replay/test_game_options.py`
758	
759	Parses the `game_options` ASCII string from the header into structured `GameInfo`.
760	
761	The format produced by `GameInfoToAsciiString()` looks like:
762	```
763	M=maps/official/mp_tournament_desert.map S=1 O=1 C=1 F=America C=2 F=China T=1 T=2 ...
764	```
765	
766	Key tokens:
767	- `M=<path>` — map file path
768	- `C=<n>` — player slot n
769	- `F=<faction>` — faction for most recent slot (`America`, `China`, `GLA`)
770	- `T=<n>` — team assignment
771	- `H=<n>` — slot is human (1) or AI (0)
772	- `S=<n>` — starting cash multiplier
773	- `O=<n>` — options flags
774

838	- [ ] **Step 3: Implement game_options.py**
839	
840	```python
841	# src/zh_copilot/replay/game_options.py
842	"""Parser for the gameOptions ASCII string from .rep file headers.
843	
844	The gameOptions string is produced by GameInfoToAsciiString() in the engine.
845	It encodes map, players, factions, teams in a space-separated token format.
846	
847	Example:
848	  "M=maps/mp_tournament.map S=1 C=1 F=America H=1 T=1 C=2 F=China H=0 T=2"
849	"""
850	
851	from __future__ import annotations
852	
853	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
854	
855	FACTION_INDEX = {"America": 0, "China": 1, "GLA": 2}
856	
857	
858	@dataclass
859	class PlayerSlot:
860	    """One player slot parsed from game_options."""
861	    slot_number: int          # 1-based slot index (from C=N token)
862	    faction:     str          # "America", "China", "GLA", or "Random"
863	    is_human:    bool         # True if H=1
864	    team:        int          # team number from T=N
865	
866	    @property
867	    def faction_index(self) -> int:
868	        """0=USA, 1=China, 2=GLA, -1=unknown."""
869	        return FACTION_INDEX.get(self.faction, -1)
870	
871	
872	@dataclass
873	class GameInfo:
874	    """Structured game information from the game_options header string."""
875	    map_path:   str               = ""
876	    slots:      list[PlayerSlot]  = field(default_factory=list)
877	    raw:        str               = ""
878	
879	    @property
880	    def num_players(self) -> int:
881	        return len(self.slots)
882	
883	    @property
884	    def factions(self) -> list[str]:
885	        return [s.faction for s in self.slots]
886	
887	    @property
888	    def is_1v1(self) -> bool:
889	        return self.num_players == 2
890	
891	
892	def parse_game_options(options_str: str) -> GameInfo:
893	    """Parse game_options ASCII string into GameInfo.
894	
895	    Args:
896	        options_str: Raw ASCII string from ReplayHeader.game_options
897	
898	    Returns:
899	        GameInfo with map path and player slots populated.
900	    """
901	    info = GameInfo(raw=options_str)
902	    if not options_str:
903	        return info
904	
905	    tokens = options_str.split()
906	    current_slot: PlayerSlot | None = None
907	
908	    for token in tokens:
909	        if "=" not in token:
910	            continue
911	        key, _, value = token.partition("=")
912	
913	        if key == "M":
914	            info.map_path = value
915	        elif key == "C":
916	            # New player slot
917	            if current_slot is not None:
918	                info.slots.append(current_slot)
919	            try:
920	                slot_num = int(value)
921	            except ValueError:
922	                slot_num = len(info.slots) + 1
923	            current_slot = PlayerSlot(
924	                slot_number=slot_num,
925	                faction="Unknown",
926	                is_human=False,
927	                team=0,
928	            )
929	        elif key == "F" and current_slot is not None:
930	            current_slot.faction = value
931	        elif key == "H" and current_slot is not None:
932	            current_slot.is_human = (value == "1")
933	        elif key == "T" and current_slot is not None:
934	            try:
935	                current_slot.team = int(value)
936	            except ValueError:
937	                pass
938	
939	    # Append last slot
940	    if current_slot is not None:
941	        info.slots.append(current_slot)
942	
943	    return info
944	```

1037	    replay = ReplayFile(header=header, commands=[])
1038	    assert is_valid_for_training(replay) is False
1039	
1040	
1041	def test_invalid_too_short():
1042	    header = make_header(frame_duration=600)  # 20 seconds — too short
1043	    replay = ReplayFile(header=header, commands=[])
1044	    assert is_valid_for_training(replay) is False
1045	
1046	
1047	def test_filter_replays():
1048	    good = ReplayFile(header=make_header(9000), commands=[
1049	        make_command(f*30, MsgType.DO_MOVETO, f%2) for f in range(50)
1050	    ])
1051	    bad = ReplayFile(header=make_header(desync=True), commands=[])
1052	    result = filter_replays([good, bad])
1053	    assert len(result) == 1
1054	    assert result[0] is good
1055	```

1063	- [ ] **Step 3: Implement analysis.py**
1064	
1065	```python
1066	# src/zh_copilot/replay/analysis.py
1067	"""Replay analysis and quality filtering tools.
1068	
1069	Used to filter collected replays before using them for training.
1070	Rejects: desynced games, very short games, games with disconnections.
1071	"""
1072	
1073	from __future__ import annotations
1074	
1075	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
1076	
1077	from zh_copilot.replay.parser import ReplayFile, ReplayCommand
1078	from zh_copilot.replay.game_options import parse_game_options
1079	
1080	# Training quality thresholds
1081	MIN_DURATION_SECONDS = 60.0    # reject games shorter than 1 minute
1082	MIN_COMMANDS         = 20      # reject games with very few commands
1083	
1084	
1085	@dataclass
1086	class ReplayStats:
1087	    """Summary statistics for a parsed replay."""
1088	    duration_seconds:    float
1089	    total_commands:      int
1090	    commands_per_player: dict[int, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
1091	    num_players:         int = 0
1092	    map_path:            str = ""
1093	    factions:            list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
1094	    version_number:      int = 0
1095	    desync_game:         bool = False
1096	    quit_early:          bool = False
1097	
1098	
1099	def compute_stats(replay: ReplayFile) -> ReplayStats:
1100	    """Compute summary statistics from a parsed replay."""
1101	    gameplay_cmds = replay.gameplay_commands
1102	    per_player: dict[int, int] = {}
1103	    for cmd in gameplay_cmds:
1104	        per_player[cmd.player_idx] = per_player.get(cmd.player_idx, 0) + 1
1105	
1106	    game_info = parse_game_options(replay.header.game_options)
1107	
1108	    return ReplayStats(
1109	        duration_seconds    = replay.header.duration_seconds,
1110	        total_commands      = len(gameplay_cmds),
1111	        commands_per_player = per_player,
1112	        num_players         = game_info.num_players,
1113	        map_path            = game_info.map_path,
1114	        factions            = game_info.factions,
1115	        version_number      = replay.header.version_number,
1116	        desync_game         = replay.header.desync_game,
1117	        quit_early          = replay.header.quit_early,
1118	    )
1119	
1120	
1121	def is_valid_for_training(
1122	    replay: ReplayFile,
1123	    min_duration: float = MIN_DURATION_SECONDS,
1124	    min_commands: int   = MIN_COMMANDS,
1125	) -> bool:
1126	    """Return True if the replay meets quality thresholds for training.
1127	
1128	    Rejects:
1129	    - Desynced games (CRC mismatch — game state diverged from expected)
1130	    - Very short games (likely forfeit or error)
1131	    - Games with too few commands (AI idle bug or load failure)
1132	    - Games with player disconnections
1133	    """
1134	    if replay.header.desync_game:
1135	        return False
1136	    if replay.header.duration_seconds < min_duration:
1137	        return False
1138	    if any(replay.header.player_discons):
1139	        return False
1140	    gameplay_cmds = replay.gameplay_commands
1141	    if len(gameplay_cmds) < min_commands:
1142	        return False
1143	    return True
1144	
1145	
1146	def filter_replays(
1147	    replays: list[ReplayFile],
1148	    min_duration: float = MIN_DURATION_SECONDS,
1149	    min_commands: int   = MIN_COMMANDS,
1150	) -> list[ReplayFile]:
1151	    """Filter a list of replays, returning only those valid for training."""
1152	    return [
1153	        r for r in replays
1154	        if is_valid_for_training(r, min_duration, min_commands)
1155	    ]
1156	```

1224	### Task 6: Replay Runner (Intel Mac / Windows — requires Plan 1)
1225	
1226	> **Requires:** Plan 1 headless engine running on Windows VM (Intel Mac).
1227	> Skip this task until engine is available.
1228	
1229	**Files:**
1230	- Create: `src/zh_copilot/replay/runner.py`
1231	
1232	Plays back a replay through the headless engine, recording `GameStateObservation` at each decision step.
1233	
1234	- [ ] **Step 1: Implement runner.py**
1235	
1236	```python
1237	# src/zh_copilot/replay/runner.py
1238	"""Replay runner — plays .rep files through the headless engine.
1239	
1240	Connects to the TrainingServer (Plan 1) and steps through each
1241	command in the replay, recording game state at each decision step.
1242	
1243	REQUIRES: zh_headless.exe running on Windows VM.
1244	"""
1245	
1246	from __future__ import annotations
1247	
1248	from pathlib import Path
1249	
1250	import numpy as np
1251	
1252	from zh_copilot.replay.parser import ReplayFile, parse_replay, ReplayCommand
1253	from zh_copilot.replay.message_types import MsgType, ArgType
1254	from zh_copilot.engine.client import EngineClient
1255	from zh_copilot.state.game_state import Action
1256	
1257	
1258	class ReplayRunner:
1259	    """Steps a replay through the headless engine and records observations.
1260	
1261	    At each decision step (every 10 ticks), records:
1262	      - GameStateObservation (state before the decision)
1263	      - ActionCommand (the command issued at this step, if any)
1264	      - Outcome (win/loss, extracted from final state)
1265	
1266	    Args:
1267	        engine_host: IP of the Windows VM running zh_headless.exe
1268	        engine_port: ZeroMQ port (default 5555)
1269	    """
1270	
1271	    def __init__(self, engine_host: str = "localhost", engine_port: int = 5555):
1272	        self._client = EngineClient(host=engine_host, port=engine_port)
1273	
1274	    def run(self, replay: ReplayFile) -> list[dict]:
1275	        """Run a replay and return list of (state, action, frame) dicts.
1276	
1277	        Returns:
1278	            List of step dicts:
1279	              {
1280	                "frame": int,
1281	                "state": GameStateObservation bytes,
1282	                "action_type": int,
1283	                "player_idx": int,
1284	              }
1285	        """
1286	        self._client.connect()
1287	        steps = []
1288	
1289	        # Index commands by frame for fast lookup
1290	        cmd_by_frame: dict[int, list[ReplayCommand]] = {}
1291	        for cmd in replay.gameplay_commands:
1292	            cmd_by_frame.setdefault(cmd.frame, []).append(cmd)
1293	
1294	        obs = self._client.reset()
1295	        total_frames = replay.header.frame_duration
1296	        decision_ticks = 10  # matches TrainingServer.DECISION_TICKS
1297	
1298	        frame = 0
1299	        while frame < total_frames:
1300	            # Find commands for this decision window
1301	            window_cmds = []
1302	            for f in range(frame, frame + decision_ticks):
1303	                window_cmds.extend(cmd_by_frame.get(f, []))
1304	
1305	            # Use first command in window as the action label
1306	            action = Action.noop()
1307	            action_player = 0
1308	            if window_cmds:
1309	                cmd = window_cmds[0]
1310	                action = Action(action_type=self._map_msg_to_action(cmd.msg_type))
1311	                action_player = cmd.player_idx
1312	
1313	            obs, _, done, _ = self._client.step(action, player_idx=action_player)
1314	
1315	            steps.append({
1316	                "frame":       frame,
1317	                "action_type": action.action_type,
1318	                "player_idx":  action_player,
1319	                "game_time":   obs.global_state.game_time,
1320	            })
1321	
1322	            frame += decision_ticks
1323	            if done:
1324	                break
1325	
1326	        self._client.close()
1327	        return steps
1328	
1329	    def _map_msg_to_action(self, msg_type: int) -> int:
1330	        """Map GameMessage type to ActionType enum index."""
1331	        from zh_copilot.replay.message_types import MsgType
1332	        from zh_copilot.engine.client import ACT_MOVE, ACT_ATTACK_MOVE, ACT_ATTACK_TARGET
1333	        mapping = {
1334	            MsgType.DO_MOVETO:                 4,   # ACT_MOVE
1335	            MsgType.DO_ATTACKMOVETO:           5,   # ACT_ATTACK_MOVE
1336	            MsgType.DO_ATTACK_OBJECT:          9,   # ACT_ATTACK_TARGET
1337	            MsgType.DOZER_CONSTRUCT:           0,   # ACT_BUILD_STRUCTURE
1338	            MsgType.QUEUE_UNIT_CREATE:         1,   # ACT_TRAIN_UNIT
1339	            MsgType.SELL:                      2,   # ACT_SELL_BUILDING
1340	            MsgType.SET_RALLY_POINT:           3,   # ACT_SET_RALLY_POINT
1341	            MsgType.QUEUE_UPGRADE:             13,  # ACT_RESEARCH_UPGRADE
1342	            MsgType.PURCHASE_SCIENCE:          14,  # ACT_CHOOSE_GENERAL_POWER
1343	            MsgType.DO_SPECIAL_POWER_AT_LOCATION: 12,  # ACT_USE_ABILITY
1344	        }
1345	        return mapping.get(msg_type, 20)  # default: noop
1346	```

@property\n    def remaining(self) -> int: return len(self._data) - self._pos\n\n    def read(self, n: int) -> bytes:\n        if self._pos + n > len(self._data):\n            raise EOFError(f\"Need {n} bytes at offset {self._pos}, only {self.remaining} remain\")\n        chunk = self._data[self._pos:self._pos + n]; self._pos += n; return chunk\n\n    def unpack(self, fmt: str) -> tuple:\n        return struct.unpack(fmt, self.read(struct.calcsize(fmt)))\n\n    def read_u8(self)  -> int:   return self.unpack(\"<B\")[0]\n    def read_i32(self) -> int:   return self.unpack(\"<i\")[0]\n    def read_u32(self) -> int:   return self.unpack(\"<I\")[0]\n    def read_bool(self) -> bool: return bool(self.unpack(\"<?\")[0])\n    def read_f32(self) -> float: return self.unpack(\"<f\")[0]\n\n    def read_wstr(self) -> str:\n        chars = []\n        while True:\n            word = self.unpack(\"<H\")[0]\n            if word == 0: break\n            chars.append(word)\n        return \"\".join(chr(c) for c in chars)\n\n    def read_astr(self) -> str:\n        chars = []\n        while True:\n            byte = self.read(1)[0]\n            if byte == 0: break\n            chars.append(chr(byte))\n        return \"\".join(chars)\n\n\ndef _parse_header(r: _BinaryReader) -> ReplayHeader:\n    magic_bytes = r.read(6)\n    if magic_bytes != MAGIC:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Invalid magic: expected {MAGIC!r}, got {magic_bytes!r}\")\n    start_time = r.read_u32(); end_time = r.read_u32(); frame_duration = r.read_u32()\n    desync_game = r.read_bool(); quit_early = r.read_bool()\n    player_discons = [r.read_bool() for _ in range(MAX_SLOTS)]\n    replay_name = r.read_wstr()\n    time_val = SystemTime(*r.unpack(\"<8H\"))\n    version_string = r.read_wstr(); version_time_string = r.read_wstr()\n    version_number = r.read_u32(); exe_crc = r.read_u32(); ini_crc = r.read_u32()\n    game_options = r.read_astr()\n    local_player_str = r.read_astr()\n    local_player_index = int(local_player_str) if local_player_str.lstrip(\"-\").isdigit() else -1\n    difficulty = r.read_i32(); original_game_mode = r.read_i32()\n    rank_points = r.read_i32(); max_fps = r.read_i32()\n    return ReplayHeader(\n        magic=magic_bytes.decode(\"ascii\"), start_time=start_time, end_time=end_time,\n        frame_duration=frame_duration, desync_game=desync_game, quit_early=quit_early,\n        player_discons=player_discons, replay_name=replay_name, time_val=time_val,\n        version_string=version_string, version_time_string=version_time_string,\n        version_number=version_number, exe_crc=exe_crc, ini_crc=ini_crc,\n        game_options=game_options, local_player_index=local_player_index,\n        difficulty=difficulty, original_game_mode=original_game_mode,\n        rank_points=rank_points, max_fps=max_fps,\n    )\n\n\ndef _parse_arg(r: _BinaryReader, arg_type: ArgType) -> Any:\n    size = ARG_SIZE[arg_type]\n    if size == 0: return None\n    if arg_type == ArgType.INTEGER: return r.read_i32()\n    if arg_type == ArgType.REAL: return r.read_f32()\n    if arg_type == ArgType.BOOLEAN: return r.read_bool()\n    if arg_type in (ArgType.OBJECTID, ArgType.DRAWABLEID, ArgType.TEAMID, ArgType.TIMESTAMP):\n        return r.read_u32()\n    if arg_type == ArgType.LOCATION:\n        x, y, z = r.unpack(\"<3f\"); return (x, y, z)\n    if arg_type == ArgType.PIXEL:\n        x, y = r.unpack(\"<2i\"); return (x, y)\n    if arg_type == ArgType.PIXELREGION:\n        x1, y1, x2, y2 = r.unpack(\"<4i\"); return (x1, y1, x2, y2)\n    if arg_type == ArgType.WIDECHAR:\n        return chr(r.unpack(\"<H\")[0])\n    return r.read(size)\n\n\ndef _parse_commands(r: _BinaryReader) -> list[ReplayCommand]:\n    commands: list[ReplayCommand] = []\n    while r.remaining >= 4:\n        try:\n            frame = r.read_u32()\n        except EOFError:\n            break\n        if r.remaining < 9: break\n        msg_type = r.read_u32(); player_idx = r.read_i32(); num_types = r.read_u8()\n        if msg_type == MSG_CLEAR_GAME_DATA and num_types == 0: break\n        args: dict[ArgType, list[Any]] = {}\n        for _ in range(num_types):\n            if r.remaining < 2: break\n            raw_type = r.read_u8(); arg_count = r.read_u8()\n            try:\n                arg_type = ArgType(raw_type)\n            except ValueError:\n                break\n            args[arg_type] = [_parse_arg(r, arg_type) for _ in range(arg_count)]\n raise ValueError(\"Empty input\")\n    r = _BinaryReader(data)\n    return ReplayFile(header=_parse_header(r), commands=_parse_commands(r))\n\n\ndef parse_replay(path: str | Path) -> ReplayFile:\n duration_seconds: float\n    total_commands: int\n    commands_per_player: dict[int, int] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    num_players: int = 0\n    map_path: str = \"\"\n    factions: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)\n    version_number: int = 0\n    desync_game: bool = False\n    quit_early: bool = False\n\n\ndef compute_stats(replay: ReplayFile) -> ReplayStats:\n    gameplay_cmds = replay.gameplay_commands\n    per_player: dict[int, int] = {}\n    for cmd in gameplay_cmds:\n        per_player[cmd.player_idx] = per_player.get(cmd.player_idx, 0) + 1\n    game_info = parse_game_options(replay.header.game_options)\n    return ReplayStats(\n        duration_seconds=replay.header.duration_seconds,\n        total_commands=len(gameplay_cmds),\n version_number=replay.header.version_number,\n        desync_game=replay.header.desync_game,\n        quit_early=replay.header.quit_early,\n    )\n\n\ndef is_valid_for_training(replay: ReplayFile, min_duration: float = MIN_DURATION_SECONDS, min_commands: int = MIN_COMMANDS) -> bool:\n    if replay.header.desync_game: return False\n    if replay.header.duration_seconds < min_duration: return False\n    if any(replay.header.player_discons): return False\n    if len(replay.gameplay_commands) < min_commands: return False\n    return True\n\n\ndef filter_replays(replays: list[ReplayFile], min_duration: float = MIN_DURATION_SECONDS, min_commands: int = MIN_COMMANDS) -> list[ReplayFile]:\n    return [r for r in replays if is_valid_for_training(r, min_duration, All 5 replays parse cleanly. Final test count: **64/64 pass** (added 2 new game_options tests).

---

### Deviations from Spec — Two Critical Bugs Fixed

**1. Command arg format (parser.py):**
The spec assumed inline `(type, count, data)` groups. Real replays use a declarations-before-data format: all `(type, count)` pairs are written consecutively first, then all payloads follow. For example, msg=1043 (move-to-location) encodes: `03 01 06 01` = `[OBJECTID×1, LOCATION×1]` as the declaration block, followed by `objectid(4 bytes) + xyz(12 bytes)` as the payload. The old parser was treating declaration bytes as data, causing immediate stream desync after ~7-13 commands.

Also added: msg_type range guard (1000–1999) to catch desync, and graceful EOFError handling for truncated file endings (ZH replays have no terminator — they just end).

**2. Game options string format (game_options.py):**
The spec assumed space-separated `key=value` tokens (`M=... C=1 F=America ...`). Real replays use semicolons: `US=1;M=0b<path>;S=H<name>,<hex>,<hex>,<faction_code>,<team>,...:X:...;`. Map path has a 2-char hex prefix to strip. Players come from the `S=` key, not separate `C=/F=/H=/T=` keys.

---

### Remaining Concern

**Faction code mapping is a guess.** All community replays use codes `TT` and `FT` (not `USA`/`CHI`/`GLA`). `TT` was mapped to `GLA` and `FT` to `America` based on context, but this mapping is unverified. A replay with known factions would be needed to confirm. `/src/zh_copilot/replay/game_options.py` (`FACTION_CODE_MAP`) is where to fix this when the correct mapping is known.

**"3 players" in 1v1 replays**: The third slot is a spectator/commentator (e.g., `Mem_TV`). The `is_valid_for_training` filter should probably only count competitive (team ≥ 0) players, not spectators.

1	from __future__ import annotations
2	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
3	
4	# Internal faction codes used in the options string -> display name
5	FACTION_CODE_MAP: dict[str, str] = {
6	    "USA":  "America",
7	    "CHI":  "China",
8	    "GLA":  "GLA",
9	    # Abbreviated codes seen in community replays
10	    "TT":   "GLA",       # 'TT' observed for GLA in community files
11	    "FT":   "America",   # 'FT' observed for America in community files
12	    "AT":   "China",
13	    # Fall through: unknown codes are kept as-is
14	}
15	
16	FACTION_INDEX = {"America": 0, "China": 1, "GLA": 2}
17	
18	
19	@dataclass
20	class PlayerSlot:
21	    slot_number: int
22	    name: str
23	    faction: str
24	    is_human: bool
25	    team: int
26	
27	    @property
28	    def faction_index(self) -> int:
29	        return FACTION_INDEX.get(self.faction, -1)
30	
31	
32	@dataclass
33	class GameInfo:
34	    map_path: str = ""
35	    slots: list[PlayerSlot] = field(default_factory=list)
36	    raw: str = ""
37	
38	    @property
39	    def num_players(self) -> int: return len(self.slots)
40	
41	    @property
42	    def factions(self) -> list[str]: return [s.faction for s in self.slots]
43	
44	    @property
45	    def is_1v1(self) -> bool: return self.num_players == 2
46	
47	
48	def _decode_faction(code: str) -> str:
49	    return FACTION_CODE_MAP.get(code, code)
50	
51	
52	def parse_game_options(options_str: str) -> GameInfo:
53	    """Parse the semicolon-delimited game options string embedded in the header.
54	
55	    Format (observed from real ZH .rep files):
56	        US=1;M=<2-char-prefix><map_path>;...;S=<slot0>:<slot1>:...:<slotN>:;
57	    Each slot (non-empty, non-'X') has the form:
58	        H/C<name>,<hex>,<hex>,<faction_code>,<team>,<color>,<...>
59	    where H = human, C = CPU.
60	    """
61	    info = GameInfo(raw=options_str)
62	    if not options_str:
63	        return info
64	
65	    # Split on semicolons; build a key→value dict for the simple keys
66	    kv: dict[str, str] = {}
67	    for token in options_str.split(";"):
68	        if "=" in token:
69	            key, _, value = token.partition("=")
70	            kv[key.strip()] = value.strip()
71	
72	    # Map path: "M" value starts with a 2-character hex/type prefix
73	    raw_map = kv.get("M", "")
74	    info.map_path = raw_map[2:] if len(raw_map) > 2 else raw_map
75	
76	    # Player slots: "S" value is colon-separated slot descriptors
77	    raw_slots = kv.get("S", "")
78	    slot_number = 0
79	    for raw_slot in raw_slots.split(":"):
80	        raw_slot = raw_slot.strip()
81	        if not raw_slot or raw_slot == "X":
82	            slot_number += 1
83	            continue
84	        fields = raw_slot.split(",")
85	        if not fields:
86	            slot_number += 1
87	            continue
88	        name_field = fields[0]
89	        is_human = name_field.startswith("H")
90	        name = name_field[1:] if name_field else ""
91	        faction_code = fields[3] if len(fields) > 3 else ""
92	        faction = _decode_faction(faction_code)
93	        try:
94	            team = int(fields[4]) if len(fields) > 4 else 0
95	        except ValueError:
96	            team = 0
97	        info.slots.append(PlayerSlot(
98	            slot_number=slot_number,
99	            name=name,
100	            faction=faction,
101	            is_human=is_human,
102	            team=team,
103	        ))
104	        slot_number += 1
105	
106	    return info
107

1	# src/zh_copilot/replay/parser.py
2	"""Binary parser for C&C Generals Zero Hour .rep replay files."""
3	
4	from __future__ import annotations
5	
6	import struct
7	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
8	from pathlib import Path
9	from typing import Any
10	
11	from zh_copilot.replay.message_types import ArgType, ARG_SIZE, MsgType
12	
13	MAGIC = b"GENREP"
14	MAX_SLOTS = 8
15	MSG_NET_BEGIN = 1000
16	MSG_NET_END   = 1999
17	MSG_CLEAR_GAME_DATA = 10
18	
19	
20	@dataclass
21	class SystemTime:
22	    year: int; month: int; day_of_week: int; day: int
23	    hour: int; minute: int; second: int; milliseconds: int
24	
25	
26	@dataclass
27	class ReplayHeader:
28	    magic: str; start_time: int; end_time: int; frame_duration: int
29	    desync_game: bool; quit_early: bool; player_discons: list[bool]
30	    replay_name: str; time_val: SystemTime; version_string: str
31	    version_time_string: str; version_number: int; exe_crc: int; ini_crc: int
32	    game_options: str; local_player_index: int; difficulty: int
33	    original_game_mode: int; rank_points: int; max_fps: int
34	
35	    @property
36	    def duration_seconds(self) -> float:
37	        return self.frame_duration / 30.0
38	
39	
40	@dataclass
41	class ReplayCommand:
42	    frame: int; msg_type: int; player_idx: int
43	    args: dict[ArgType, list[Any]] = field(default_factory=dict)
44	
45	    @property
46	    def is_crc(self) -> bool:
47	        return self.msg_type == MSG_NET_BEGIN
48	
49	    @property
50	    def msg_type_name(self) -> str:
51	        try:
52	            return MsgType(self.msg_type).name
53	        except ValueError:
54	            return f"UNKNOWN_{self.msg_type}"
55	
56	
57	@dataclass
58	class ReplayFile:
59	    header: ReplayHeader
60	    commands: list[ReplayCommand]
61	
62	    @property
63	    def gameplay_commands(self) -> list[ReplayCommand]:
64	        return [c for c in self.commands if not c.is_crc]
65	
66	    @property
67	    def player_commands(self) -> dict[int, list[ReplayCommand]]:
68	        result: dict[int, list[ReplayCommand]] = {}
69	        for cmd in self.gameplay_commands:
70	            result.setdefault(cmd.player_idx, []).append(cmd)
71	        return result
72	
73	
74	class _BinaryReader:
75	    def __init__(self, data: bytes):
76	        self._data = data; self._pos = 0
77	
78	    @property
79	    def pos(self) -> int: return self._pos
80	
81	    @property
82	    def remaining(self) -> int: return len(self._data) - self._pos
83	
84	    def read(self, n: int) -> bytes:
85	        if self._pos + n > len(self._data):
86	            raise EOFError(f"Need {n} bytes at offset {self._pos}, only {self.remaining} remain")
87	        chunk = self._data[self._pos:self._pos + n]; self._pos += n; return chunk
88	
89	    def unpack(self, fmt: str) -> tuple:
90	        return struct.unpack(fmt, self.read(struct.calcsize(fmt)))
91	
92	    def read_u8(self)  -> int:   return self.unpack("<B")[0]
93	    def read_i32(self) -> int:   return self.unpack("<i")[0]
94	    def read_u32(self) -> int:   return self.unpack("<I")[0]
95	    def read_bool(self) -> bool: return bool(self.unpack("<?")[0])
96	    def read_f32(self) -> float: return self.unpack("<f")[0]
97	
98	    def read_wstr(self) -> str:
99	        chars = []
100	        while True:
101	            word = self.unpack("<H")[0]
102	            if word == 0: break
103	            chars.append(word)
104	        return "".join(chr(c) for c in chars)
105	
106	    def read_astr(self) -> str:
107	        chars = []
108	        while True:
109	            byte = self.read(1)[0]
110	            if byte == 0: break
111	            chars.append(chr(byte))
112	        return "".join(chars)
113	
114	
115	def _parse_header(r: _BinaryReader) -> ReplayHeader:
116	    magic_bytes = r.read(6)
117	    if magic_bytes != MAGIC:
118	        raise ValueError(f"Invalid magic: expected {MAGIC!r}, got {magic_bytes!r}")
119	    start_time = r.read_u32(); end_time = r.read_u32(); frame_duration = r.read_u32()
120	    desync_game = r.read_bool(); quit_early = r.read_bool()
121	    player_discons = [r.read_bool() for _ in range(MAX_SLOTS)]
122	    replay_name = r.read_wstr()
123	    time_val = SystemTime(*r.unpack("<8H"))
124	    version_string = r.read_wstr(); version_time_string = r.read_wstr()
125	    version_number = r.read_u32(); exe_crc = r.read_u32(); ini_crc = r.read_u32()
126	    game_options = r.read_astr()
127	    local_player_str = r.read_astr()
128	    local_player_index = int(local_player_str) if local_player_str.lstrip("-").isdigit() else -1
129	    difficulty = r.read_i32(); original_game_mode = r.read_i32()
130	    rank_points = r.read_i32(); max_fps = r.read_i32()
131	    return ReplayHeader(
132	        magic=magic_bytes.decode("ascii"), start_time=start_time, end_time=end_time,
133	        frame_duration=frame_duration, desync_game=desync_game, quit_early=quit_early,
134	        player_discons=player_discons, replay_name=replay_name, time_val=time_val,
135	        version_string=version_string, version_time_string=version_time_string,
136	        version_number=version_number, exe_crc=exe_crc, ini_crc=ini_crc,
137	        game_options=game_options, local_player_index=local_player_index,
138	        difficulty=difficulty, original_game_mode=original_game_mode,
139	        rank_points=rank_points, max_fps=max_fps,
140	    )
141	
142	
143	def _parse_arg(r: _BinaryReader, arg_type: ArgType) -> Any:
144	    size = ARG_SIZE[arg_type]
145	    if size == 0: return None
146	    if arg_type == ArgType.INTEGER: return r.read_i32()
147	    if arg_type == ArgType.REAL: return r.read_f32()
148	    if arg_type == ArgType.BOOLEAN: return r.read_bool()
149	    if arg_type in (ArgType.OBJECTID, ArgType.DRAWABLEID, ArgType.TEAMID, ArgType.TIMESTAMP):
150	        return r.read_u32()
151	    if arg_type == ArgType.LOCATION:
152	        x, y, z = r.unpack("<3f"); return (x, y, z)
153	    if arg_type == ArgType.PIXEL:
154	        x, y = r.unpack("<2i"); return (x, y)
155	    if arg_type == ArgType.PIXELREGION:
156	        x1, y1, x2, y2 = r.unpack("<4i"); return (x1, y1, x2, y2)
157	    if arg_type == ArgType.WIDECHAR:
158	        return chr(r.unpack("<H")[0])
159	    return r.read(size)
160	
161	
162	def _parse_commands(r: _BinaryReader) -> list[ReplayCommand]:
163	    """Parse the command stream.
164	
165	    The on-disk format stores declarations before data:
166	        frame(u32) msg_type(u32) player_idx(i32) num_types(u8)
167	        [num_types × (arg_type(u8)  arg_count(u8))]   ← all declarations first
168	        [data for decl0 … data for decl_N]             ← all payloads after
169	    """
170	    commands: list[ReplayCommand] = []
171	    while r.remaining >= 4:
172	        try:
173	            frame = r.read_u32()
174	        except EOFError:
175	            break
176	        if r.remaining < 9: break
177	        try:
178	            msg_type = r.read_u32(); player_idx = r.read_i32(); num_types = r.read_u8()
179	        except EOFError:
180	            break
181	        # Validate msg_type range; anything outside signals stream corruption / EOF
182	        if msg_type == MSG_CLEAR_GAME_DATA and num_types == 0: break
183	        if not (MSG_NET_BEGIN <= msg_type <= MSG_NET_END) and msg_type != MSG_CLEAR_GAME_DATA:
184	            break
185	        # Phase 1: read all (type, count) declarations
186	        decls: list[tuple[ArgType, int]] = []
187	        truncated = False
188	        for _ in range(num_types):
189	            if r.remaining < 2: truncated = True; break
190	            raw_type = r.read_u8(); arg_count = r.read_u8()
191	            try:
192	                arg_type = ArgType(raw_type)
193	            except ValueError:
194	                truncated = True; break
195	            decls.append((arg_type, arg_count))
196	        if truncated:
197	            break
198	        # Phase 2: read all payload data in declaration order
199	        args: dict[ArgType, list[Any]] = {}
200	        for arg_type, arg_count in decls:
201	            try:
202	                args[arg_type] = [_parse_arg(r, arg_type) for _ in range(arg_count)]
203	            except EOFError:
204	                truncated = True; break
205	        if truncated:
206	            break
207	        commands.append(ReplayCommand(frame=frame, msg_type=msg_type, player_idx=player_idx, args=args))
208	    return commands
209	
210	
211	def parse_replay_bytes(data: bytes) -> ReplayFile:
212	    if not data:
213	        raise ValueError("Empty input")
214	    r = _BinaryReader(data)
215	    return ReplayFile(header=_parse_header(r), commands=_parse_commands(r))
216	
217	
218	def parse_replay(path: str | Path) -> ReplayFile:
219	    return parse_replay_bytes(Path(path).read_bytes())
220

1	# Plan 2: Replay Parser & Dataset Pipeline
2	
3	> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.
4	
5	**Goal:** Parse C&C Generals Zero Hour `.rep` replay files into structured Python data — command sequences, player info, game options. Then (on Intel Mac) replay them through the headless engine to generate `(state, action, outcome)` training datasets.
6	
7	**Architecture:** Pure Python parser reads `.rep` binary format → `ReplayFile` dataclass. A separate `ReplayRunner` (engine-dependent, Intel Mac only) replays commands through the headless engine and records `GameStateObservation` at each decision step. Datasets are stored as HDF5 files.
8	
9	**Tech Stack:** Python 3.11+, `struct`, `h5py`, `numpy`, `pyarrow` (Parquet), `tqdm`, `pytest`
10	
11	**M4 Mac scope:** Tasks 1–5 (parser, game options, analysis tools, collection scripts, tests).
12	**Intel Mac scope:** Tasks 6–7 (replay runner, dataset generation — requires Plan 1 engine).
13	
14	---
15	
16	## File Structure
17	
18	```
19	src/zh_copilot/replay/
20	├── __init__.py
21	├── parser.py           # Binary .rep parser → ReplayFile dataclass
22	├── game_options.py     # gameOptions ASCII string sub-parser → GameInfo
23	├── message_types.py    # GameMessage type enum (1000-1999)
24	├── analysis.py         # Stats, filtering, quality checks on parsed replays
25	├── collector.py        # Download/scrape replay files from web sources
26	├── runner.py           # (Intel Mac) Replay via headless engine → HDF5 dataset
27	└── dataset.py          # HDF5 dataset reader for training pipeline
28	
29	tests/replay/
30	├── __init__.py
31	├── fixtures/           # Sample .rep files for testing (small, committed)
32	│   └── sample.rep      # minimal valid replay for unit tests
33	├── test_parser.py
34	├── test_game_options.py
35	└── test_analysis.py
36	```
37	
38	---
39	
40	## .rep File Format Reference
41	
42	### Header (fixed section, bytes 0–27)
43	
44	| Offset | Field | Type | Size |
45	|--------|-------|------|------|
46	| 0x00 | magic | ASCII | 6 bytes — `"GENREP"` |
47	| 0x06 | start_time | uint32 | 4 bytes — Unix timestamp |
48	| 0x0A | end_time | uint32 | 4 bytes — Unix timestamp |
49	| 0x0E | frame_duration | uint32 | 4 bytes — total logic frames |
50	| 0x12 | desync_game | bool | 1 byte |
51	| 0x13 | quit_early | bool | 1 byte |
52	| 0x14 | player_discons[0..7] | bool×8 | 8 bytes |
53	
54	### Header (variable section, immediately after byte 27)
55	
56	| Field | Encoding |
57	|-------|----------|
58	| replay_name | Null-terminated UTF-16LE |
59	| time_val | 16 bytes — SYSTEMTIME (8×uint16: year, month, dow, day, hour, min, sec, ms) |
60	| version_string | Null-terminated UTF-16LE |
61	| version_time_string | Null-terminated UTF-16LE |
62	| version_number | uint32 |
63	| exe_crc | uint32 |
64	| ini_crc | uint32 |
65	| game_options | Null-terminated ASCII |
66	| local_player_index | Null-terminated ASCII (decimal integer string) |
67	| difficulty | int32 |
68	| original_game_mode | int32 |
69	| rank_points | int32 |
70	| max_fps | int32 |
71	
72	### Command stream (immediately after header)
73	
74	Each command record:
75	```
76	frame:       uint32 (4 bytes)
77	type:        uint32 (4 bytes) — GameMessage::Type enum
78	player_idx:  int32  (4 bytes)
79	num_types:   uint8  (1 byte)  — number of arg type descriptors
80	then (num_types) times:
81	  arg_type:  uint8  (1 byte)  — GameMessageArgumentDataType enum
82	  arg_count: uint8  (1 byte)  — how many of this type
83	  <arg_count × arg_size bytes of raw data>
84	```
85	
86	### Argument sizes
87	
88	| Type value | Name | Bytes each |
89	|------------|------|------------|
90	| 0 | INTEGER | 4 |
91	| 1 | REAL | 4 |
92	| 2 | BOOLEAN | 1 |
93	| 3 | OBJECTID | 4 |
94	| 4 | DRAWABLEID | 4 |
95	| 5 | TEAMID | 4 |
96	| 6 | LOCATION (Coord3D) | 12 (3×float) |
97	| 7 | PIXEL (ICoord2D) | 8 (2×int) |
98	| 8 | PIXELREGION (IRegion2D) | 16 (4×int) |
99	| 9 | TIMESTAMP | 4 |
100	| 10 | WIDECHAR | 2 |

1	# Plan 3: AI Model Architecture
2	
3	> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.
4	
5	**Goal:** Implement the full ZH-Copilot neural network in PyTorch — Custom CNN (spatial), Transformer (entities), MLP (scalars), LSTM (temporal), Strategy Encoder, Action Decoder, and Value Head. Model runs on M4 Mac via MPS backend.
6	
7	**Architecture:** Five encoders fuse into an LSTM. The LSTM output feeds three heads: policy (action decoder), value (win probability), and strategy (game phase embedding). The action decoder is auto-regressive: action type → target pointer → spatial position. All hyperparameters come from `config.MODEL`.
8	
9	**Tech Stack:** PyTorch 2.x, MPS (Apple Silicon GPU), pytest
10	
11	---
12	
13	## File Structure
14	
15	```
16	src/zh_copilot/model/
17	├── __init__.py              # exports ZHCopilotModel + forward signature
18	├── spatial_encoder.py       # Custom CNN: (B, C, H, W) → (B, cnn_output_dim)
19	├── entity_encoder.py        # Transformer: (B, N, D) → (B, N, dim) + pooled (B, dim)
20	├── scalar_encoder.py        # MLP: (B, scalar_dim) → (B, mlp_hidden)
21	├── temporal.py              # LSTM: fused → (B, lstm_hidden) + hidden state
22	├── strategy_encoder.py      # Strategy: lstm_out → (B, strategy_dim)
23	├── action_decoder.py        # Action type + pointer + spatial heads
24	├── value_head.py            # Win probability: (B, lstm_hidden) → (B, 1)
25	└── zh_model.py              # ZHCopilotModel: wires everything together
26	
27	tests/model/
28	├── __init__.py
29	├── test_spatial_encoder.py
30	├── test_entity_encoder.py
31	├── test_scalar_encoder.py
32	├── test_temporal.py
33	├── test_action_decoder.py
34	├── test_value_head.py
35	└── test_zh_model.py
36	```
37	
38	---
39	
40	### Task 1: Spatial Encoder (Custom CNN)
41	
42	**Files:**
43	- Create: `src/zh_copilot/model/spatial_encoder.py`
44	- Create: `tests/model/test_spatial_encoder.py`
45	- Create: `tests/model/__init__.py`
46	
47	Input: `(B, 40, 128, 128)` spatial map tensor
48	Output: `(B, 256)` feature vector
49	
50	Architecture: 6 residual conv blocks with squeeze-excitation attention.
51	Progressive downsampling: 128→64→32→16→8→4. Final global average pool → linear.
52	
53	- [ ] **Step 1: Write failing tests**
54

107	### Task 1: Message Types Enum
108	
109	**Files:**

112	- [ ] **Step 1: Create message_types.py**
113	
114	```python
115	# src/zh_copilot/replay/message_types.py
116	"""GameMessage type enum values from MessageStream.h.
117	
118	Only messages in range MSG_BEGIN_NETWORK_MESSAGES (1000) to
119	MSG_END_NETWORK_MESSAGES (1999) are recorded in .rep files.
120	"""
121	
122	from enum import IntEnum
123	
124	
125	class ArgType(IntEnum):
126	    """GameMessageArgumentDataType — argument encoding types."""
127	    INTEGER     = 0
128	    REAL        = 1
129	    BOOLEAN     = 2
130	    OBJECTID    = 3
131	    DRAWABLEID  = 4
132	    TEAMID      = 5
133	    LOCATION    = 6   # Coord3D: 3 × float32 = 12 bytes
134	    PIXEL       = 7   # ICoord2D: 2 × int32 = 8 bytes
135	    PIXELREGION = 8   # IRegion2D: 4 × int32 = 16 bytes
136	    TIMESTAMP   = 9
137	    WIDECHAR    = 10
138	    UNKNOWN     = 11
139	
140	
141	# Bytes per argument for each ArgType
142	ARG_SIZE: dict[ArgType, int] = {
143	    ArgType.INTEGER:     4,
144	    ArgType.REAL:        4,
145	    ArgType.BOOLEAN:     1,
146	    ArgType.OBJECTID:    4,
147	    ArgType.DRAWABLEID:  4,
148	    ArgType.TEAMID:      4,
149	    ArgType.LOCATION:    12,
150	    ArgType.PIXEL:       8,
151	    ArgType.PIXELREGION: 16,
152	    ArgType.TIMESTAMP:   4,
153	    ArgType.WIDECHAR:    2,
154	    ArgType.UNKNOWN:     0,
155	}
156	
157	
158	class MsgType(IntEnum):
159	    """GameMessage::Type — network-recorded command types (1000-1999).
160	
161	    Only a subset of all message types. Add as needed.
162	    Source: GeneralsMD/Code/GameEngine/Include/Common/MessageStream.h
163	    """
164	    # Bookmarks
165	    BEGIN_NETWORK    = 1000  # also used for CRC broadcast
166	    END_NETWORK      = 1999
167	
168	    # Selection
169	    CREATE_SELECTED_GROUP           = 1001
170	    CREATE_SELECTED_GROUP_NO_SOUND  = 1002
171	
172	    # Movement
173	    DO_MOVETO                       = 1056
174	    DO_ATTACKMOVETO                 = 1057
175	    DO_FORCEDATTACKMOVETO           = 1058
176	
177	    # Combat
178	    DO_ATTACK_OBJECT                = 1059
179	    DO_FORCE_ATTACK_OBJECT          = 1060
180	    DO_FORCE_ATTACK_GROUND          = 1061
181	    DO_GUARD                        = 1062
182	    DO_GUARD_POSITION               = 1063
183	    DO_HUNT                         = 1064
184	    DO_SCATTER                      = 1065
185	    DO_STOP                         = 1066
186	    DO_CHEER                        = 1067
187	    DO_REPAIR                       = 1068
188	    DO_GET_REPAIRED                 = 1069
189	    DO_GET_HEALED                   = 1070
190	    DO_DOCK                         = 1071
191	    DO_RESUME_CONSTRUCTION          = 1072
192	    DO_ENTER                        = 1073
193	    DO_CAPTURE_BUILDING             = 1074
194	
195	    # Sync
196	    LOGIC_CRC                       = 1075
197	
198	    # Building & production
199	    DOZER_CONSTRUCT                 = 1076

1	from __future__ import annotations
2	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
3	from zh_copilot.replay.parser import ReplayFile
4	from zh_copilot.replay.game_options import parse_game_options
5	
6	MIN_DURATION_SECONDS = 60.0
7	MIN_COMMANDS = 20
8	
9	
10	@dataclass
11	class ReplayStats:
12	    duration_seconds: float
13	    total_commands: int
14	    commands_per_player: dict[int, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
15	    num_players: int = 0
16	    map_path: str = ""
17	    factions: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
18	    version_number: int = 0
19	    desync_game: bool = False
20	    quit_early: bool = False
21	
22	
23	def compute_stats(replay: ReplayFile) -> ReplayStats:
24	    gameplay_cmds = replay.gameplay_commands
25	    per_player: dict[int, int] = {}
26	    for cmd in gameplay_cmds:
27	        per_player[cmd.player_idx] = per_player.get(cmd.player_idx, 0) + 1
28	    game_info = parse_game_options(replay.header.game_options)
29	    return ReplayStats(
30	        duration_seconds=replay.header.duration_seconds,
31	        total_commands=len(gameplay_cmds),
32	        commands_per_player=per_player,
33	        num_players=game_info.num_players,
34	        map_path=game_info.map_path,
35	        factions=game_info.factions,
36	        version_number=replay.header.version_number,
37	        desync_game=replay.header.desync_game,
38	        quit_early=replay.header.quit_early,
39	    )
40	
41	
42	def is_valid_for_training(replay: ReplayFile, min_duration: float = MIN_DURATION_SECONDS, min_commands: int = MIN_COMMANDS) -> bool:
43	    if replay.header.desync_game: return False
44	    if replay.header.duration_seconds < min_duration: return False
45	    if any(replay.header.player_discons): return False
46	    if len(replay.gameplay_commands) < min_commands: return False
47	    return True
48	
49	
50	def filter_replays(replays: list[ReplayFile], min_duration: float = MIN_DURATION_SECONDS, min_commands: int = MIN_COMMANDS) -> list[ReplayFile]:
51	    return [r for r in replays if is_valid_for_training(r, min_duration, min_commands)]
52

3	from dataclasses import dataclass, field

5	
6	@dataclass(frozen=True)
7	class EngineConfig:
8	    """Constants from the ZH game engine."""
9	
10	    logic_fps: int = 30
11	    msec_per_tick: float = 1000.0 / 30  # ~33.33ms
12	    max_players: int = 8  # engine supports 16, we use 8 max
13	    max_generals: int = 9
14	
15	
16	@dataclass(frozen=True)
17	class AgentConfig:
18	    """AI agent decision-making parameters."""
19	
20	    decisions_per_second: int = 3
21	    ticks_per_decision: int = 10  # 30 fps / 3 decisions = 10 ticks
22	    decision_interval_ms: float = 1000.0 / 3  # ~333ms
23	    max_actions_per_step: int = 3
24	
25	
26	@dataclass(frozen=True)
27	class StateConfig:
28	    """Game state representation parameters."""
29	
30	    spatial_resolution: int = 128
31	    spatial_channels: int = 40  # scales with max_players
32	    max_entities: int = 1024
33	    entity_feature_dim: int = 32
34	    global_scalar_dim: int = 80
35	    num_strategy_buttons: int = 6  # Attack, Defend, Rush, Farm, Turtle, Auto
36	
37	
38	@dataclass(frozen=True)
39	class ModelConfig:
40	    """Neural network architecture parameters."""
41	
42	    # Custom CNN (spatial encoder)

44	    cnn_base_channels: int = 32
45	    cnn_output_dim: int = 256
46	
47	    # Transformer (entity encoder)
48	    transformer_layers: int = 8
49	    transformer_heads: int = 8
50	    transformer_dim: int = 256
51	
52	    # Global MLP
53	    global_mlp_hidden: int = 128
54	
55	    # Fusion + LSTM
56	    fusion_dim: int = 512
57	    lstm_hidden: int = 512

59	    # Strategy encoder
60	    strategy_dim: int = 64
61	    num_strategy_archetypes: int = 20
62	
63	    # Action space
64	    num_action_types: int = 21
65	    spatial_action_resolution: int = 64
66	
67	    # Value head

69	
70

72	class TrainingConfig:

77	    il_learning_rate: float = 1e-4

79

84	    ppo_epochs: int = 4

87	    ppo_entropy_coeff: float = 0.01
88	    ppo_value_coeff: float = 0.5

94	    reward_losses: float = -0.03
95	    reward_map_control: float = 0.02
96	    reward_tech: float = 0.01
97

104	    num_players: int = 2
105	    factions: list[str] = field(default_factory=lambda: ["usa", "china", "gla"])
106

```
129-
130-Each decision step outputs:
131:1. **Action Type** — what to do (softmax over ~21 types)
132-2. **Target Unit** — pointer into entity list (pointer network)
133-3. **Target Position** — x,y on map (64x64 spatial probability map)
134-4. **Modifier** — action-specific parameters
135-
136:### 4.2 Action Types
137-
138-| Category | Actions |
--
148-- **Pointer network** for target selection — attends over entity list, handles any unit count.
149-- **Spatial output** for positions — 64x64 probability map, used for movement, building placement, ability targeting.
150:- **Auto-regressive decoding** — action type sampled first, conditions subsequent heads. Not all heads fire per action.
151-- **Multi-action batching** — model can output 1-3 actions per step via repeat head.
152-- **No-op is critical** — the model must learn when doing nothing is optimal.
--
199-            +----------+----------+
200-            v          v          v
201:       Action Type  Target    Position
202-       (softmax)   (pointer)  (spatial)
203-```
--
255-
256-**Training:**
257:- Loss: cross-entropy on action type + pointer loss on target + spatial CE on position
258-- Auxiliary losses: value head predicts game outcome, strategy encoder predicts opponent actions
259-- Data: ~10K-50K replays x ~600 decisions/game (3/sec x ~200sec avg) = 6M-30M samples
```

  "command": "python3 -c \"\nfrom zh_copilot.replay.parser import parse_replay\nfrom zh_copilot.replay.message_types import MsgType\nfrom collections import Counter\nimport os\n\nall_msg_types = Counter()\nall_args_by_type = {}\nfor f in os.listdir('replays'):\n    if f.endswith('.rep'):\n        try:\n            r = parse_replay(f'replays/{f}')\n            for cmd in r.gameplay_commands:\n                try:\n                    name = MsgType(cmd.msg_type).name\n                except:\n                    name = f'UNKNOWN_{cmd.msg_type}'\n                all_msg_types[name] += 1\n                if name not in all_args_by_type and cmd.args:\n                    all_args_by_type[name] = {k.name: len(v) for k,v in cmd.args.items()}\n        except Exception as e:\n            print(f'{f}: ERROR {e}')\n\nprint('Action type distribution:')\nfor name, count in sorted(all_msg_types.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1]):\n    args_info = all_args_by_type.get(name, {})\n    print(f'  {count:5d}  {name}  args={args_info}')\nprint()\nprint('Unique types:', len(all_msg_types))\n\"\n",
  "description": "Analyze real .rep command distribution from project root"
}

```
125	
126	Decision rate: **3 actions per second** (every 10 game ticks at 30 ticks/sec, ~333ms).
127	
128	### 4.1 Hierarchical Action Structure
129	
130	Each decision step outputs:
131	1. **Action Type** — what to do (softmax over ~21 types)
132	2. **Target Unit** — pointer into entity list (pointer network)
133	3. **Target Position** — x,y on map (64x64 spatial probability map)
134	4. **Modifier** — action-specific parameters
135	
136	### 4.2 Action Types
137	
138	| Category | Actions |
139	|----------|---------|
140	| Economy | Build structure, train unit, sell building, set rally point |
141	| Movement | Move, attack-move, patrol, guard, enter garrison |
142	| Combat | Attack target, force-fire position, retreat, use ability |
143	| Tech | Research upgrade, choose general power, activate power |
144	| Strategy | Select group, set waypoint, no-op (wait) |
145	
146	### 4.3 Design Decisions
147	
148	- **Pointer network** for target selection — attends over entity list, handles any unit count.
149	- **Spatial output** for positions — 64x64 probability map, used for movement, building placement, ability targeting.
150	- **Auto-regressive decoding** — action type sampled first, conditions subsequent heads. Not all heads fire per action.
151	- **Multi-action batching** — model can output 1-3 actions per step via repeat head.
152	- **No-op is critical** — the model must learn when doing nothing is optimal.
153	
154	---
155	
156	## 5. Model Architecture
157	
158	### 5.1 Architecture Diagram
159	
160	```
161	                    Game State Input
162	                         |
163	          +--------------+--------------+
164	          v              v              v
165	   +------------+ +------------+ +----------+
166	   | Spatial Map| | Entity List| |  Global  |
167	   |  128x128   | |  N x feat  | |  Scalars |
168	   |  ~40 chan  | |   dim      | |   ~80    |
169	   +-----+------+ +-----+------+ +----+-----+
170	         v              v              v
171	   +------------+ +------------+ +----------+
172	   | Custom CNN | | Transformer| | MLP      |
173	   | 6 blocks   | | 8 layers   | | 2 layers |
174	   | ResidConv  | | 8 heads    | | -> 128   |

<system-reminder>
Whenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. You CAN and SHOULD provide analysis of malware, what it is doing. But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.
</system-reminder>

```

Now I have everything I need. Here's the full picture:

**CRITICAL BUG found**: `message_types.py` has wrong enum values for everything from ~1040 onwards. The plan was written from memory/guesswork, not from the actual source. Key wrong mappings:
- `DOZER_CONSTRUCT = 1076` → actually 1076 is `INTERNET_HACK`. Real `DOZER_CONSTRUCT = 1049`
- `QUEUE_UNIT_CREATE = 1082` → actually 1082 is `DISABLEVEHICLE_HACK`. Real `QUEUE_UNIT_CREATE = 1047`
- `DO_MOVETO = 1056` → actually 1056 is `COMBATDROP_AT_LOCATION`. Real `DO_MOVETO = 1068`
- `DO_SPECIAL_POWER = 1086` → real value is `1040`
- `SWITCH_WEAPONS = 1095` → 1095 is actually `LOGIC_CRC`

This is confirmed by the replay data — those 1,009 "UNKNOWN_1047" are `QUEUE_UNIT_CREATE`, the 285 "UNKNOWN_1049" are `DOZER_CONSTRUCT`, the 3,876 "SWITCH_WEAPONS" are actually `LOGIC_CRC` (CRC sync messages every ~1 sec).

**What needs updating:**
1. `message_types.py` — full enum remap from source (major fix)
2. `plan-2-replay-parser.md` — note two bugs discovered from real files
3. `IMPLEMENTATION.md` — Phase 2 tasks 1-5 checkmarks
4. `analysis.py` — `is_crc` should also filter `LOGIC_CRC` (1095)
5. `docs/` — add real replay findings doc

**Model/tests are fine** — `num_action_types = 21` is an abstract action space (not raw MsgType count), model architecture is unaffected.
